8365 — resistance band pass through

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8365 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A band stretch that moves overhead and behind you, opening up the shoulders and chest.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold the band in front of your thighs with a wide grip and straight arms. Stand tall.

8366 — kettlebell side swing v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8366 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A swing performed to the side of the body that hits the obliques and hips while the legs drive the kettlebell.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet wider than hips and hold a kettlebell with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 8366.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with feet wider than hips and hold a kettlebell with both hands.

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 8366.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with feet wider than hips and hold a kettlebell with both hands. 8366.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge at the hips and swing the bell between your legs. 8366.json#/instructions/1
  3. Drive your hips forward and swing it up and across to one side. 8366.json#/instructions/2
  4. Let it fall back through your legs and swing to the other side. 8366.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your back flat throughout. 8366.json#/instructions/4

8369 — seated over head straight arm stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8369 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated overhead reach that stretches the chest and shoulders. It helps open up the upper body after desk time.

Proposed staging

  6. Release slowly. 8369.json#/instructions/5

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8371 after decompression) · Source record

Proposed staging

8374 — step out from squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8374 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat that steps one leg out to the side before returning. It works the legs and hips with a bit of lateral movement.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet together.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 8374.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 8374.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet together.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 8374.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 8374.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet together. 8374.json#/instructions/0
  2. Squat down by pushing your hips back. 8374.json#/instructions/1
  3. Stand up while stepping one foot out to the side. 8374.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bring that foot back in and squat again. 8374.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate sides each rep. 8374.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your chest up and knees tracking over your toes. 8374.json#/instructions/5

  • Bring that foot back in and squat again. Step 4; 8374.json#/instructions/3

8375 — lever angled leg press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8375 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A plate-loaded angled press that works the thighs and glutes with a stable seated position.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit in the machine with your back flat on the pad and feet on the platform shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 8375.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 8375.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 8375.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit in the machine with your back flat on the pad and feet on the platform shoulder-width apart. 8375.json#/instructions/0
  2. Release the safety handles. 8375.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the platform until your knees are bent about 90 degrees. 8375.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press through your whole foot until your legs are nearly straight. 8375.json#/instructions/3
  5. Do not lock your knees out. 8375.json#/instructions/4

8399 — side floor tap plank

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8399 after decompression) · Source record

A plank where you tap a hand to the floor at the side, challenging the obliques and anti-rotation control.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 8423 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated lean back that wakes up the abs by holding the torso against gravity.

Proposed staging

  4. Lean back slowly while keeping your back straight. 8423.json#/instructions/3

9180 — stick standing shoulder mobilization in extension

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 9180 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Holding a stick behind your back, you lift it away to open the front of the shoulders. It improves shoulder extension.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall holding a stick behind your back with an overhand grip.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall holding a stick behind your back with an overhand grip. 9180.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your arms straight. 9180.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lift the stick back and away from your body. 9180.json#/instructions/2
  4. Stay tall and avoid leaning forward. 9180.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for a moment. 9180.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower slowly and repeat. 9180.json#/instructions/5
